- **phi**: Use when φ must be read as the self-address constant — the number that is its own fold, computed not stored. Where pi is a finite seed unfolding to an infinite tail, φ is the fixed point: x = 1 + 1/x, the value the fold returns unchanged. Iterate the fold from any seed and it converges to φ; Fibonacci ratios sharpen toward it at every step. The arithmetic instance of concept.self.address. ([erpax/erpax](https://github.com/erpax/erpax/tree/HEAD/src/phi) · ★2)
